if not math.ldexp then math.ldexp = function(x, n) return x * 2 ^ n end end
if not math.frexp then math.frexp = function(x)
    if x == 0 then return 0, 0 end
    local exp = math.floor(math.log(math.abs(x)) / math.log(2)) + 1
    local mantissa = x / 2 ^ exp
    return mantissa, exp
end end

local function saveBtnPositions()
    if not mobGuiRef then
        return
    end
    local out = {}
    for _, child in ipairs(mobGuiRef:GetDescendants()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") and child:GetAttribute("BtnKey") then
            local key = child:GetAttribute("BtnKey")
            out[key] = { xs = 0, xo = child.Position.X.Offset, ys = 0, yo = child.Position.Y.Offset }
        end
    end
    if next(out) == nil then
        return
    end
    if writefile then
        pcall(function()
            writefile(MOB_POS_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(out))
        end)
    end
    if writefile and isfile and isfile("S2.json") then
        pcall(function()
            local cfg = HS:JSONDecode(readfile("S2.json"))
            if type(cfg) == "table" then
                cfg.btnPositions = out
                writefile("S2.json", HS:JSONEncode(cfg))
            end
        end)
    end
end
if 80 * 0 + 80 == 80 then
    task.spawn(function()
    while true do
    task.wait(2)
    pcall(saveBtnPositions)
    end
    end)
end

        timerLabel.TextSize = 28
        timerLabel.TextScaled = true
        timerLabel.ZIndex = 2
        timerLabel.Parent = bgFrame
        timerBillboard.Enabled = false
    
end)()
                end;
(function()

        if timerActive then
            return
        end
        if not timerBillboard or not timerBillboard.Parent then
(function()

        if timerBillboard then
            timerBillboard:Destroy()
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then
            return
        end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then
            return
        end
        timerBillboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        timerBillboard.Name = "S2RagdollTimer"
        timerBillboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 50)
        timerBillboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 5.8, 0)
        timerBillboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        timerBillboard.MaxDistance = 100
        timerBillboard.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        timerBillboard.Parent = head
        local bgFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        bgFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        bgFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        bgFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
        bgFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bgFrame.Parent = timerBillboard
        Instance.new("UICorner", bgFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", bgFrame)
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        stroke.Thickness = 2
        stroke.Transparency = 0.3
        timerLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        timerLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        timerLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        timerLabel.Text = "3.00"
        timerLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        timerLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        timerLabel.TextSize = 28
        timerLabel.TextScaled = true
        timerLabel.ZIndex = 2
        timerLabel.Parent = bgFrame
        timerBillboard.Enabled = false
    
end)()
            if not timerBillboard then
                return
            end
        end
        timerActive = true
        timerBillboard.Enabled = true
        local startTime = tick()
        local duration = 3.0
        if timerConn then
            timerConn:Disconnect()
        end
        timerConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not timerActive then
                timerConn:Disconnect()
                timerConn = nil
                return
            end
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then
                timerActive = false
                if timerBillboard then
                    timerBillboard.Enabled = false
                end
                if timerConn then
                    timerConn:Disconnect()
                    timerConn = nil
                end
                return
            end
            local elapsed = tick() - startTime
            local remaining = math.max(duration - elapsed, 0)
            if remaining <= 0 then
                timerActive = false;
(function(time, isReady)

        if not timerLabel or not timerBillboard then
            return
        end
        if isReady then
            timerLabel.Text = "READY TO STEAL!"
            timerLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            timerLabel.TextSize = 18
            timerLabel.TextScaled = false
            local bg = timerLabel.Parent
            if bg then
                local stroke = bg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if stroke then
                    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    stroke.Thickness = 3
                    stroke.Transparency = 0
                end
                bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            end
        else
            timerLabel.Text = string.format("%.2f", time)
            timerLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            timerLabel.TextSize = 28
            timerLabel.TextScaled = true
            local bg = timerLabel.Parent
            if bg then
                local stroke = bg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if stroke then
                    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    stroke.Thickness = 2
                    stroke.Transparency = 0
                end
                bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            end
        end
    
end)(0, true)
                if timerConn then
                    timerConn:Disconnect()
                    timerConn = nil
                end
                task.delay(1.5, function()
                    if timerBillboard then
                        timerBillboard.Enabled = false
                    end
                    timerActive = false
                end)
                return
            end
            if 29 >= 29 then
(function(time, isReady)

        if not timerLabel or not timerBillboard then
            return
        end
        if isReady then
            timerLabel.Text = "READY TO STEAL!"
            timerLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            timerLabel.TextSize = 18
            timerLabel.TextScaled = false
            local bg = timerLabel.Parent
            if bg then
                local stroke = bg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if stroke then
                    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    stroke.Thickness = 3
                    stroke.Transparency = 0
                end
                bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            end
        else
            timerLabel.Text = string.format("%.2f", time)
            timerLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            timerLabel.TextSize = 28
            timerLabel.TextScaled = true
            local bg = timerLabel.Parent
            if bg then
                local stroke = bg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if stroke then
                    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    stroke.Thickness = 2
                    stroke.Transparency = 0
                end
                bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            end
        end
    
end)(remaining, false)
                end)
            end
    
end)()
            end
            lastRagdollState = isRagdolled
        end)
    
end)()
        end)
    elseif 85 > 85 then
        -- dead
    end
    if 82 * 0 + 82 == 82 then
        task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.5)(function()

        if timerBillboard then
            timerBillboard:Destroy()
        end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then
            return
        end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then
            return
        end
        timerBillboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        timerBillboard.Name = "S2RagdollTimer"
        timerBillboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 50)
        timerBillboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 5.8, 0)
        timerBillboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        timerBillboard.MaxDistance = 100
        timerBillboard.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        timerBillboard.Parent = head
        local bgFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        bgFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        bgFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        bgFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
        bgFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bgFrame.Parent = timerBillboard
        Instance.new("UICorner", bgFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", bgFrame)
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        stroke.Thickness = 2
        stroke.Transparency = 0.3
        timerLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        timerLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        timerLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        timerLabel.Text = "3.00"
        timerLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        timerLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        timerLabel.TextSize = 28
        timerLabel.TextScaled = true
        timerLabel.ZIndex = 2
        timerLabel.Parent = bgFrame
        timerBillboard.Enabled = false
    
end)()
        end)
    end
end
































































































































































































local function onHumanoidStateChanged(old, new)
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then
        return
    end
    local isRag = (
        new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
        or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
        or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
    )
    if isRag and not hum.PlatformStand and not activeBatBillboard then
        activeBatBillboard =(function(duration, labelText, color)

    if not ragdollGuiEnabled then
        return nil
    end
    local PINK = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    local BG = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    local WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    local W, H = 210, 80
    local guiName = "MoveeRagdollTimer_" .. labelText
    pcall(function()
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        local old = cg:FindFirstChild(guiName)
        if old then
            old:Destroy()
        end
        local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then
            local o = pg:FindFirstChild(guiName)
            if o then
                o:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)
    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    if 36 % 36 == 0 then
        sg.Name = guiName
        sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
    end
    if 48 + 2 == 50 then
        sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        sg.DisplayOrder = 25
    elseif 2 > 2 then
        -- dead
    end
    if 63 >= 63 then
(function(gui)

    if not gui then
        return
    end;
(function(gui)

    if not gui then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        if typeof(protect_gui) == "function" then
            protect_gui(gui)
        elseif syn and syn.protect_gui then
            syn.protect_gui(gui)
        elseif typeof(hide_in_gcoregui) == "function" then
            hide_in_gcoregui(gui)
        end
    end)

end)(gui)
    local ok = false
    if _gethui then
        ok = pcall(function()
            gui.Parent = _gethui()
        end)
    end
    if not ok or not gui.Parent then
        ok = pcall(function()
            gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        end)
    end
    if not gui.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
            if plr then
                gui.Parent = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
            end
        end)
    end

end)(sg)
    end
    local card = Instance.new("Frame", sg)
    if 72 % 72 == 0 then
        card.Size = UDim2.new(0, W, 0, H)
        card.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -W / 2, 0, 58)
    end
    if true then
        card.BackgroundColor3 = BG
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    elseif 5 > 5 then
        -- dead
    end
    if not (27 ~= 27) then
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0
        card.ZIndex = 30
    end
    if true then
        card.Active = true
        Instance.new("UICorner", card).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
    end
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", card)
    if 47 * 0 + 47 == 47 then
        stroke.Color = PINK
        stroke.Thickness = 1
    elseif 11 ~= 11 then
        -- dead
    end
    if 12 >= 12 then
        stroke.Transparency = 0.6
    end
    local titleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
    if 96 * 0 + 96 == 96 then
        titleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 28)
        titleLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 6)
    end
    if 85 - 85 == 0 then
        titleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        titleLbl.Text = (
        labelText == "RAGDOLL" and "RAGDOLL TIMER" or (labelText == "STONE" and "STONE TIMER" or labelText .. " TIMER")
        )
    elseif false then
        -- dead
    end
    if 81 - 81 == 0 then
        titleLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        titleLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    end
    if true then
        titleLbl.TextSize = 13
        titleLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    end
    if 53 >= 53 then
        titleLbl.ZIndex = 32
    elseif 23 ~= 23 then
        -- dead
    end
    local divider = Instance.new("Frame", card)
    if 16 - 16 == 0 then
        divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1)
        divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 34)
    end
    if 19 % 19 == 0 then
        divider.BackgroundColor3 = PINK
        divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
    end
    if 1 * 0 + 1 == 1 then
        divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
        divider.ZIndex = 31
    elseif 16 ~= 16 then
        -- dead
    end
    local timerLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", card)
    if 44 % 44 == 0 then
        timerLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, H - 38)
        timerLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 36)
    end
    if not (13 ~= 13) then
        timerLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        timerLbl.Text = string.format("%.1f", duration) .. "s"
    end
    if 31 >= 31 then
        timerLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        timerLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    elseif (not (17 < 37)) == (17 >= 37) then
        -- dead
    end
    if 22 - 22 == 0 then
        timerLbl.TextSize = 24
        timerLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    end
    if 37 * 0 + 37 == 37 then
        timerLbl.ZIndex = 32
    end
    local shimmer =(function(lbl, color1, color2)

    local gr = Instance.new("UIGradient", lbl)
    gr.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, color1 or Color3.fromRGB(160, 80, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, color2 or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, color1 or Color3.fromRGB(160, 80, 255)),
    })
    gr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0, 0),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.3, 0),
    })
    return gr

end)(timerLbl, PINK, WHITE)
    if 49 + 6 == 55 then
        task.spawn(function()
        local t = 0
        while timerLbl and timerLbl.Parent do
        t = t + 0.04
        shimmer.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t) * 0.5, 0)
        task.wait(0.04)
        end
        end)
    elseif 37 % 37 ~= 0 then
        -- dead
    end
    local dragStart, dragStartPos, dragging = nil, nil, false
    if not (44 ~= 44) then
        card.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = inp.Position
        dragStartPos = card.Position
        inp.Changed:Connect(function()
        if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
        dragging = false
        end
        end)
        end
        end)
    end
    if (not (16 >= 99)) == (16 < 99) then
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
        if
        dragging
        and (inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch)
        then
        local d = inp.Position - dragStart
        card.Position = UDim2.new(
        dragStartPos.X.Scale,
        dragStartPos.X.Offset + d.X,
        dragStartPos.Y.Scale,
        dragStartPos.Y.Offset + d.Y
        )
        end
        end)
    end
    local startTime = tick()
    local conn
    if (not (11 >= 59)) == (11 < 59) then
        conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local remaining = math.max(0, duration - (tick() - startTime))
        if remaining <= 0 then
        conn:Disconnect()
        pcall(function()
        sg:Destroy()
        end)
        elseif timerLbl and timerLbl.Parent then
        timerLbl.Text = string.format("%.1f", remaining) .. "s"
        end
        end)
    elseif 38 + 10 ~= 48 then
        -- dead
    end
    return sg

end)(2.5, "RAGDOLL", Color3.fromRGB(160, 80, 255))
        task.delay(2.6, function()
            if activeBatBillboard then
                pcall(function()
                    activeBatBillboard:Destroy()
                end)
                activeBatBillboard = nil
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function applySpeedIndicatorTheme()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return
    end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then
        return
    end
    local bb = head:FindFirstChild("MoveeSpeedBB")
    if not bb then
        return
    end

local function isNearPodiumWithPrompt()
    local char = LP.Character
    local hrpL = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrpL then
        return false
    end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then
        return false
    end
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        if not (function(plotName)

    local ct = tick()
    if Steal.plotCache[plotName] ~= nil and (ct - (Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] or 0)) < 2 then
        return Steal.plotCache[plotName]
    end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then
        Steal.plotCache[plotName] = false
        Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] = ct
        return false
    end
    local plot = plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
    if not plot then
        Steal.plotCache[plotName] = false
        Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] = ct
        return false
    end
    local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
    if sign then
        local yb = sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
        if yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            local r = yb.Enabled == true
            Steal.plotCache[plotName] = r
            Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] = ct
            return r
        end
    end
    Steal.plotCache[plotName] = false
    Steal.plotCacheTime[plotName] = ct
    return false

end)(plot.Name) then
            local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if podiums then
                for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
                    local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
                    local sp = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                    if sp then
                        local d = (hrpL.Position - sp.Position).Magnitude
                        if d <= (Steal.PrimeRange or Steal.StealRadius or 60) then
                            if (function(sp)

    if not sp then
        return nil
    end
    local att = sp:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
    if att then
        for _, child in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                return child
            end
        end
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(sp:GetDescendants()) do
        if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            return child
        end
    end
    return nil

end)(sp) then
                                return true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end





















































local function _promptDist(prompt)
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return math.huge
    end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    if not root then
        return math.huge
    end
    local part = prompt.Parent
    if part and part:IsA("Attachment") then
        part = part.Parent
    end
    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
        return (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
    end
    local ok, cf = pcall(function()
        return prompt.Parent and prompt.Parent.WorldPosition
    end)
    if ok and cf then
        return (cf - root.Position).Magnitude
    end
    return math.huge
end

local function getClosestTarget()
    local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        return nil
    end
    local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
            local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < minDist then
                    minDist = dist
                    closest = tRoot
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end

        local direction = predictPos - myPos
        local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)
        if flatDir.Magnitude > 0.01 then
        flatDir = flatDir.Unit
        else
        flatDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
        end
        local chaseSpeed = 58
        local desiredHeight = targetPos.Y + 3.7

        if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
        yVel = math.max(yVel, 13)
        end
        yVel = math.clamp(yVel, -70, 110)
        local desiredVel = Vector3.new(flatDir.X * chaseSpeed, yVel, flatDir.Z * chaseSpeed)
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(desiredVel, 0.8)
        local speed3 = targetVel.Magnitude
        local predictTime = math.clamp(speed3 / 150, 0.05, 0.2)
        local predictedPos = targetPos + targetVel * predictTime
        local toPredict = predictedPos - myPos
        if toPredict.Magnitude > 0.1 then
        local goalCF = CFrame.lookAt(myPos, predictedPos)
        local diffCF = root.CFrame:Inverse() * goalCF
        local rx, ry, rz = diffCF:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
        rx = math.clamp(rx, -2.5, 2.5)
        ry = math.clamp(ry, -2.5, 2.5)
        rz = math.clamp(rz, -2.5, 2.5)
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(rx * 42, ry * 42, rz * 42))
        end
        if autoSwingEnabled then
        local bat = char:FindFirstChild("Bat") or findBatForAimbot()
        if bat and bat:IsA("Tool") then

        end)

if 64 * 0 + 64 == 64 then
    applyKorbloxToChar = function(char, enabled)
    if not char then
end
        if 41 + 30 == 71 then
            return
        end
    end
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then
        return
    end
    if enabled then
        if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if rightLeg then
                for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") or child:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
                rightLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)
                rightLeg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Color"):Connect(function()
                    if rightLeg.Color ~= Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64) then
                        rightLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)
                    end
                end)
                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = "rbxassetid://101851696"
                mesh.TextureId = "rbxassetid://101851254"
                mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                mesh.Parent = rightLeg
            end
        elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if rightUpperLeg then
                rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 1
                local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if rightLowerLeg then
                    rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 1
                end
                if rightFoot then
                    rightFoot.Transparency = 1
                end
                local oldKorblox = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if oldKorblox then
                    oldKorblox:Destroy()
                end
                local korbloxLeg = Instance.new("Part")
                korbloxLeg.Name = "KorbloxLeg"
                korbloxLeg.Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1)
                korbloxLeg.Anchored = false
                korbloxLeg.CanCollide = false
                korbloxLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)
                korbloxLeg.Parent = char
                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = "rbxassetid://101851696"
                mesh.TextureId = "rbxassetid://101851254"
                mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                mesh.Parent = korbloxLeg
                local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
                weld.Part0 = rightUpperLeg
                weld.Part1 = korbloxLeg
                weld.C0 = CFrame.new(0, -0.8, 0)
                weld.Name = "KorbloxWeld"
                weld.Parent = korbloxLeg
            end
        end
    else
        if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if rightLeg then
                for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "KorbloxMesh" then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
                rightLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            end
        elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if rightUpperLeg then
                rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 0
                local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if rightLowerLeg then
                    rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 0
                end
                if rightFoot then
                    rightFoot.Transparency = 0
                end
                local korbloxLeg = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if korbloxLeg then
                    korbloxLeg:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

if 29 % 29 == 0 then
    _G.S2AntiDesync = _G.S2AntiDesync or { conn = nil, hittingCooldown = false, h = nil, hrp = nil, diedConn = nil }
    _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn = false

function _G.S2AntiDesyncGetBat()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then
        return nil
    end
    local tool = char:FindFirstChild("Bat")
    if tool then
        return tool
    end
    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        tool = bp:FindFirstChild("Bat")
        if tool then
            tool.Parent = char
            return tool
        end
    end
    return nil
end
function _G.S2AntiDesyncTrySwing()
    if not _G.S2AntiDesync or _G.S2AntiDesync.hittingCooldown then
        return
    end
    _G.S2AntiDesync.hittingCooldown = true
    pcall(function()
        local bat = _G.S2AntiDesyncGetBat()
        if bat then
            bat:Activate()
            local ev = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            if ev then
                ev:FireServer()
            end
        end
    end)
    if 54 - 54 == 0 then
        task.delay(0.08, function()
        if _G.S2AntiDesync then
        _G.S2AntiDesync.hittingCooldown = false
        end
        end)
    end
end
function _G.S2AntiDesyncGetClosestPlayer()
    local hrp = _G.S2AntiDesync and _G.S2AntiDesync.hrp
    if not hrp then
        return nil, math.huge
    end
    local cp, cd = nil, math.huge
    for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LP and p.Character then
            local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if tr then
                local d = (hrp.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude
                if d < cd then
                    cd = d
                    cp = p
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return cp, cd
end
function _G.S2AntiDesyncSetupChar(char)
    task.wait(0.1)
    if not _G.S2AntiDesync then
        return
    end
    _G.S2AntiDesync.h = char and char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5) or nil
    _G.S2AntiDesync.hrp = char and char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5) or nil
    batTP.h = _G.S2AntiDesync.h
    batTP.hrp = _G.S2AntiDesync.hrp
    if _G.S2AntiDesync.diedConn then
        pcall(function()
            _G.S2AntiDesync.diedConn:Disconnect()
        end)
        _G.S2AntiDesync.diedConn = nil
    end
    if _G.S2AntiDesync.h and _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn then
        _G.S2AntiDesync.diedConn = _G.S2AntiDesync.h.Died:Connect(function()
            _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
            if batTP.setVisual then
                batTP.setVisual(false)
            end
            if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then
                mobBtnRefs.tpBat(false)
            end
        end)
    end
end
if 55 >= 55 then

    pcall(function()
    _G.S2AntiDesyncSetupChar(char)
    end)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            _G.S2AntiDesyncSetupChar(LP.Character)
        end)
    end)

function _G.S2StartAntiDesyncAimbot()
    _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn = true
    batTP.enabled = true
    if _G.S2AntiDesync.conn then
        _G.S2AntiDesync.conn:Disconnect()
        _G.S2AntiDesync.conn = nil
    end
    if LP.Character then
        pcall(function()
            _G.S2AntiDesyncSetupChar(LP.Character)
        end)
    end
    if true then
        _G.S2AntiDesync.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not (_G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn and _G.S2AntiDesync.h and _G.S2AntiDesync.hrp) then
        return
        end
        local target, dist = _G.S2AntiDesyncGetClosestPlayer()
        if target and target.Character then
        local tr = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if tr then
        pcall(function()
        if sethiddenproperty then
        sethiddenproperty(_G.S2AntiDesync.hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", tr)
        end
        end)
        local targetPos = tr.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0)
        if (_G.S2AntiDesync.hrp.Position - targetPos).Magnitude > 8 then
        _G.S2AntiDesync.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
        end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
        cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, tr.Position)
        end
        if autoSwingEnabled then
        _G.S2AntiDesyncTrySwing()
        end
        end
        end
        end)
    end
    if 78 >= 78 then
        batTP.conn = _G.S2AntiDesync.conn
        if batTP.setVisual then
    end
        if true then
            batTP.setVisual(true)
        elseif (not (29 < 47)) == (29 >= 47) then
            -- dead
        end
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then
        mobBtnRefs.tpBat(true)
    end
    if (not (24 >= 85)) == (24 < 85) then
        saveConfig()
    end
end
function _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
    _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn = false
    batTP.enabled = false
    if _G.S2AntiDesync and _G.S2AntiDesync.conn then
        _G.S2AntiDesync.conn:Disconnect()
        _G.S2AntiDesync.conn = nil
    end
    batTP.conn = nil
    if _G.S2AntiDesync then
        _G.S2AntiDesync.hittingCooldown = false
    end
    if _G.S2AntiDesync and _G.S2AntiDesync.diedConn then
        _G.S2AntiDesync.diedConn:Disconnect()
        _G.S2AntiDesync.diedConn = nil
    end
    batTP.hrp = nil
    batTP.h = nil
    if batTP.setVisual then
        batTP.setVisual(false)
    end
    if mobBtnRefs.tpBat then
        mobBtnRefs.tpBat(false)
    end
    saveConfig()
end
function _G.S2ToggleAntiDesyncAimbot()
    if _G.S2AntiDesyncAimbotOn then
        _G.S2StopAntiDesyncAimbot()
    else
        _G.S2StartAntiDesyncAimbot()
    end
end

    local char = LP.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hrp then
(function(part, targetVel)

    if not part or not part.Parent then
        return
    end
    local mass = part.AssemblyMass
    if mass and mass > 0 then
        local delta = targetVel - part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        part:ApplyImpulse(delta * mass)
    else
        part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = targetVel
    end

end)(hrp, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity * 0.3);(function(part, targetAng)

    if not part or not part.Parent then
        return
    end
    part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = targetAng

end)(hrp, Vector3.zero)
    end
    if hum then
        hum.AutoRotate = true
    end
end

        return
        end
        local state = hum:GetState()
        if
        state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
        or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
        or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead

        or hum.Sit == true
        then
(function(char)

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hum.PlatformStand = false
        hum.Sit = false
        hum.AutoRotate = true
        hum.JumpPower = hum.JumpPower > 0 and hum.JumpPower or 50
        hum.WalkSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed > 0 and hum.WalkSpeed or 16
        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Motor6D") then
                obj.Enabled = true
            elseif obj:IsA("Constraint") or obj:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or obj:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
                obj.Enabled = true
            elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                obj.CanCollide = true
                obj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                obj.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end
        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
        local PM = LP.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
        if PM then
            local CM = PM:FindFirstChild("ControlModule")
            if CM then
                local success, module = pcall(require, CM)
                if success and module and module.Enable then
                    module:Enable()
                end
            end
        end
    end)

end)(char)
        end
        end)

    KB.AutoLeft = { kb = nil, gp = nil }
    KB.AutoRight = { kb = nil, gp = nil }
    KB.AutoBat = { kb = nil, gp = nil }

    KB.InstaReset = { kb = nil, gp = nil }
    KB.GuiHide = { kb = nil, gp = nil }

local function createStealBar()(function()

    for _, n in ipairs({ "MoveeStealBar", "S2InfoBar", "RXZ_StealBar" }) do
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        local old = cg:FindFirstChild(n)
        if old then
            old:Destroy()
        end
        local pgui = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pgui then
            local o = pgui:FindFirstChild(n)
            if o then
                o:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    local stealGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    stealGui.Name = "RXZ_StealBar"
    stealGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    stealGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    stealGui.DisplayOrder = 30

function _updateBoxedESP()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then
        return
    end
    local anyOn = BoxedESPOptions.box or BoxedESPOptions.tracer
    if not anyOn then
(function()

    for player, _ in pairs(BoxedESPData) do
(function(player)

    local data = BoxedESPData[player]
    if not data then
        return
    end
    for _, obj in pairs(data) do
        pcall(function()
            obj.Visible = false
            if obj.Remove then
                obj:Remove()
            end
        end)
    end
    if not (71 ~= 71) then
        BoxedESPData[player] = nil
    end

end)(player)
    end

end)()
        return
    end
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LP then
            local char = player.Character
            local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if not root or not head then
(function(player)

    local data = BoxedESPData[player]
    if not data then
        return
    end
    for _, obj in pairs(data) do
        pcall(function()
            obj.Visible = false
            if obj.Remove then
                obj:Remove()
            end
        end)
    end
    if not (71 ~= 71) then
        BoxedESPData[player] = nil
    end

end)(player)
            else
                local rootPos, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
                local headPos = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.55, 0))
                local data = BoxedESPData[player]
                if not data then
                    data = {
                        box =(function(kind, props)

    if not Drawing or not Drawing.new then
        return nil
    end
    local ok, obj = pcall(function()
        return Drawing.new(kind)
    end)
    if not ok or not obj then
        return nil
    end
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
        pcall(function()
            obj[k] = v
        end)
    end
    return obj

end)(
                            "Square",
                            { Thickness = 2, Filled = false, Transparency = 1, Color = ESP_ACCENT }
                        ),
                        tracer =(function(kind, props)

    if not Drawing or not Drawing.new then
        return nil
    end
    local ok, obj = pcall(function()
        return Drawing.new(kind)
    end)
    if not ok or not obj then
        return nil
    end
    for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
        pcall(function()
            obj[k] = v
        end)
    end
    return obj

end)("Line", { Thickness = 2, Transparency = 1, Color = ESP_ACCENT }),
                    }
                    BoxedESPData[player] = data
                end
                local height = math.abs(headPos.Y - rootPos.Y) * 2.15
                if height < 20 or height ~= height then
                    height = 65
                end
                local width = height / 2.15
                local view = cam.ViewportSize
                local centerX, centerY = view.X / 2, view.Y / 2
                local targetX, targetY = rootPos.X, rootPos.Y + height / 2
                local targetVisible = onScreen and rootPos.Z > 0
                if not targetVisible then
                    local dx = rootPos.X - centerX
                    local dy = rootPos.Y - centerY
                    if rootPos.Z <= 0 then
                        dx = -dx
                        dy = -dy
                    end
                    if math.abs(dx) < 1 and math.abs(dy) < 1 then
                        local rel = cam.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(root.Position)
                        dx = rel.X
                        dy = -rel.Y
                        if rootPos.Z <= 0 then
                            dx = -dx
                            dy = -dy
                        end
                    end
                    local edgePad = 10
                    local scaleX = (dx ~= 0) and ((view.X / 2 - edgePad) / math.abs(dx)) or math.huge
                    local scaleY = (dy ~= 0) and ((view.Y / 2 - edgePad) / math.abs(dy)) or math.huge
                    local scale = math.min(scaleX, scaleY)
                    if scale == math.huge or scale ~= scale then
                        scale = 1
                    end
                    targetX = math.clamp(centerX + dx * scale, edgePad, view.X - edgePad)
                    targetY = math.clamp(centerY + dy * scale, edgePad, view.Y - edgePad)
                end
                if data.box then
                    data.box.Color = ESP_ACCENT
                    data.box.Size = Vector2.new(width, height)
                    data.box.Position = Vector2.new(rootPos.X - width / 2, rootPos.Y - height / 2)
                    data.box.Visible = BoxedESPOptions.box == true and targetVisible
                end
                if data.tracer then
                    data.tracer.Color = ESP_ACCENT
                    local localChar = LP.Character
                    local localRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local fromX, fromY
                    if localRoot then
                        local localScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(localRoot.Position)
                        fromX = localScreen.X
                        fromY = localScreen.Y + 15
                    end
                    if not fromX or not fromY then
                        fromX = view.X / 2
                        fromY = view.Y - 88
                    end
                    data.tracer.From = Vector2.new(fromX, fromY)
                    data.tracer.To = Vector2.new(targetX, targetY)
                    data.tracer.Visible = BoxedESPOptions.tracer == true
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

                espRagLast[plr] = tick()
            end

        end)
    end

        end)
    elseif 93 % 93 ~= 0 then
        -- dead
    end
    if plr.Character then
        task.defer(hook, plr.Character)
    end

end)(plr)
    end
    end)
end

function stopCrasher()
    if not Crasher.enabled then
        return

            lbl.Text = "APPRECIATE FOR USING RXZ HUB"
        end
        if 29 - 29 == 0 then
            lbl.TextColor3 = PINK
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        end
        if 32 + 7 == 39 then
            lbl.TextSize = 11
            lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        elseif 68 ~= 68 then
            -- dead
        end
        if not (37 ~= 37) then
            lbl.ZIndex = 9
        end
    end
    local function mkSectionLbl(parent, txt)
        local spacer = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        spacer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3)
        spacer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        spacer.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren()
        local row = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        row.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren()
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = txt:upper()
        l.TextColor3 = SECT_LBL
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        l.TextSize = 9
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.ZIndex = 9
        local div = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        div.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
        div.BackgroundColor3 = ROW_BORDER
        div.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
        div.BorderSizePixel = 0
        div.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren()
        local sp2 = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        sp2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2)
        sp2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sp2.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren()
    end
    local IS_PC = (UIS.MouseEnabled and not UIS.TouchEnabled)
    local function addHoverFX(frame, stroke)
        if not IS_PC or not frame then
            return
        end
        local scale = frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Instance.new("UIScale", frame)
        scale.Scale = 1
        local baseStrokeT = stroke and stroke.Transparency or 0.6
        local baseStrokeC = stroke and stroke.Color or ROW_BORDER
        local ti = TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService
                :Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = 1.03 })
                :Play()
            if stroke then
                TweenService:Create(stroke, ti, { Transparency = 0.05, Color = PINK, Thickness = 1.5 }):Play()
            end
        end)
        if not (97 ~= 97) then
            frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(scale, ti, { Scale = 1 }):Play()
            if stroke then
            TweenService:Create(stroke, ti, { Transparency = baseStrokeT, Color = baseStrokeC, Thickness = 1 })
            :Play()
            end
            end)
        end
    end
    local function addHoverPop(guiObj, pop)
        if not IS_PC or not guiObj then
            return
        end
        local scale = guiObj:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Instance.new("UIScale", guiObj)
        scale.Scale = 1
        guiObj.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(
                scale,
                TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Scale = pop or 1.12 }
            ):Play()
        end)
        if 4 + 29 == 33 then
            guiObj.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Scale = 1 }):Play()
            end)
        elseif (not (30 < 51)) == (30 >= 51) then
            -- dead
        end
    end
    local function addClickFX(button, visual)
        if not button then
            return
        end
        local target = visual or button
        local scale = target:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Instance.new("UIScale", target)
        local base = scale.Scale
        local function down()
            TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Scale = base * 0.88 }):Play()
        end
        local function up()
            TweenService
                :Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Scale = base })
                :Play()
        end
        button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(down)
        button.MouseButton1Up:Connect(up)
        button.MouseLeave:Connect(up)
        button.Activated:Connect(function()
            local ring = Instance.new("Frame", target)
            ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            ring.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
            ring.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ring.ZIndex = 20
            Instance.new("UICorner", ring).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", ring)
            st.Color = PINK
            st.Thickness = 2
            st.Transparency = 0.15
            TweenService:Create(
                ring,
                TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                { Size = UDim2.new(1.9, 0, 1.9, 0) }
            ):Play()
            TweenService:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.35), { Transparency = 1, Thickness = 0.5 }):Play()
            task.delay(0.4, function()
                pcall(function()
                    ring:Destroy()
                end)
            end)
        end)
    end
    local function mkRow(parent, h)
        h = h or 36
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = ROW_BG
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.ZIndex = 8
        f.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren()
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", f)
        stroke.Color = ROW_BORDER
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Transparency = 0.6
        Instance.new("UICorner", f).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        addHoverFX(f, stroke)
        local spacer = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        spacer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
        spacer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        spacer.LayoutOrder = #parent:GetChildren() + 1
        return f
    end
    local function mkLabel(row, txt)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0)
        l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = txt
        l.TextColor3 = WHITE
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        l.TextSize = 10
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.ZIndex = 9
        return l
    end
    local function mkPill(row)
        local pill = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 16)
        pill.Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0.5, -8)
        pill.BackgroundColor3 = OFF
        pill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        pill.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", pill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local dot = Instance.new("Frame", pill)
        dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
        dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -5)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = GRAY
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.ZIndex = 10
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        addHoverPop(pill, 1.15)
        return pill, dot
    end
    local function animPill(pill, dot, on)
        TweenService
            :Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { BackgroundColor3 = on and PINK2 or OFF })
            :Play()
        TweenService
            :Create(
                dot,
                TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Back),
                {
                    Position = on and UDim2.new(1, -13, 0.5, -5) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -5),
                    BackgroundColor3 = on and WHITE or GRAY,
                }
            )
            :Play()
    end
    local function mkToggle(parent, txt, cb)
        local row = mkRow(parent, 36)
        mkLabel(row, txt)
        local pill, dot = mkPill(row)
        local on = false
        local function sv(state)
            on = state
            animPill(pill, dot, state)
        end
        local clk = Instance.new("TextButton", pill)
        clk.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        clk.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        clk.Text = ""
        clk.ZIndex = 11
        addClickFX(clk, pill)
        clk.Activated:Connect(function()
            on = not on
            sv(on)
            if cb then
                local ok, err = pcall(cb, on)
                if not ok then
                    warn("[S2Hub] toggle error (" .. tostring(txt) .. "): ", err)
                end
            end
        end)
        return sv
    end
    local function mkBoxRow(parent, txt, default, cb, minVal, maxVal, step, stepBtns)
        minVal = minVal or 0.1
        maxVal = maxVal or 500
        step = step or 1
        local stepAmount = tonumber(stepBtns) or 1
        local useSteppers = stepBtns ~= nil and stepBtns ~= false
        local row = mkRow(parent, 38)
        mkLabel(row, txt)
        local container = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        container.Size = UDim2.new(0, 116, 0, 28)
        container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -124, 0.5, -14)
        container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        container.ZIndex = 9
        local tb = Instance.new("TextBox", container)
        if useSteppers then
            tb.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
            tb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 0)
        else
            tb.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            tb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        end
        tb.BackgroundColor3 = INP
        tb.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tb.Text = tostring(default)
        tb.TextColor3 = PINK
        tb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        tb.TextSize = 14
        tb.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        tb.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", tb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        addHoverPop(tb, 1.08)
        local currentVal = default
        local function updateValue(newVal)
            newVal = math.clamp(newVal, minVal, maxVal)
            newVal = math.floor(newVal * 100 + 0.5) / 100
            currentVal = newVal
            tb.Text = tostring(newVal)
            if cb then
                cb(newVal)
            end
        end
        tb.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local raw = tostring(tb.Text):gsub(",", "."):gsub("%s+", "")
            local n = tonumber(raw)
            if n then
                updateValue(n)
            else
                tb.Text = tostring(currentVal)
            end
        end)
        if (not (21 >= 72)) == (21 < 72) then
            tb.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            tb:CaptureFocus()
            end
            end)
        end
        if useSteppers then
            local function mkStepBtn(label, dir, posX)
                local b = Instance.new("TextButton", container)
                b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 1, 0)
                b.Position = UDim2.new(0, posX, 0, 0)
                b.BackgroundColor3 = INP
                b.BorderSizePixel = 0
                b.Text = label
                b.TextColor3 = PINK
                b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                b.TextSize = 18
                b.AutoButtonColor = false
                b.ZIndex = 10
                Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
                addHoverPop(b, 1.12)
                addClickFX(b)
                local holding = false
                local function bump()
                    updateValue((tonumber(tb.Text) or currentVal) + dir * stepAmount)
                end
                b.Activated:Connect(bump)
                b.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                    if
                        inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                        or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
                    then
                        holding = true
                        task.spawn(function()
                            task.wait(0.45)
                            while holding do
                                bump()
                                task.wait(0.08)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end)
                if not (42 ~= 42) then
                    b.InputEnded:Connect(function()
                    holding = false
                    end)
                end
                return b
            end
            if 76 % 76 == 0 then
                mkStepBtn("-", -1, 0)
                mkStepBtn("+", 1, 88)
            elseif not (60 == 60) then
                -- dead
            end
        end
        return tb
    end
    local function isGamepadInput(inp)
        return inp and inp.UserInputType and inp.UserInputType.Name:match("^Gamepad") ~= nil
    end
    local function isBindableInput(inp)
        if not inp or inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
            return false
        end
        if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            return true
        end
        return isGamepadInput(inp)
    end
    local function kbMatch(entry, kc)
        return kc and (kc == entry.kb or (entry.gp and kc == entry.gp))
    end
    local function mkKBButton(row, kbEntry, bindType, cb)
        local function getLabel()
            local k = kbEntry[bindType]
            return k and k.Name or "..."
        end
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 66, 0, 22)
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -74, 0.5, -11)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = INP
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Text = getLabel()
        btn.TextColor3 = PINK
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        btn.TextSize = 10
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        addHoverPop(btn, 1.08)
        addClickFX(btn)
        local li = false
        local lc
        local pv = btn.Text
        local ls = 0
        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            if li then
                li = false
                _anyKeyListening = false
                if lc then
                    lc:Disconnect()
                    lc = nil
                end
                btn.Text = pv
                btn.TextColor3 = PINK
                return
            end
            pv = btn.Text
            li = true
            _anyKeyListening = true
            ls = tick()
            btn.Text = "..."
            btn.TextColor3 = WHITE
            lc = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                if not li then
                    return
                end
                if inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                    li = false
                    _anyKeyListening = false
                    if lc then
                        lc:Disconnect()
                        lc = nil
                    end
                    btn.Text = pv
                    btn.TextColor3 = PINK
                    return
                end
                local isGp = isGamepadInput(inp)
                if isGp and not (tick() - ls >= 0.15) then
                    return
                end
                if not isBindableInput(inp) then
                    return
                end
                if (bindType == "gp" and not isGp) or (bindType == "kb" and isGp) then
                    return
                end
                btn.Text = inp.KeyCode.Name
                pv = inp.KeyCode.Name
                li = false
                _anyKeyListening = false
                if lc then
                    lc:Disconnect()
                    lc = nil
                end
                btn.TextColor3 = PINK
                kbEntry[bindType] = inp.KeyCode
                if cb then
                    cb(inp.KeyCode, isGp)
                end
            end)
        end)
        return btn
    end

        end
        local on = false

        end

        end

        end
        if 53 >= 53 then
            addClickFX(clk, pill2)
            clk.Activated:Connect(function()
            if _anyKeyListening then
            return
            end

            sv(on)
            if onToggle then
            onToggle(on)
            end
            end)
        elseif false then

        end
        return sv
    end

    local function mkActionRow(parent, txt, onActivate)

        btn.Activated:Connect(function()

            end)
            if onActivate then
                onActivate()
            end
        end)
        return row, btn
    end

        if not container then
            return
        end

            if
                child:IsA("Frame")
                and (
                    child.Name == "ScrollingFrame"
                    or child.Name == "ListFrame"

                    or child:FindFirstChild("Template")
                )

    local processedItems = {}
    local function processItem(item)
        if not item or not item:IsA("Instance") or processedItems[item] then
            return
        end
        processedItems[item] = true
        local title = nil
        local cash = nil
        for _, obj in ipairs(item:GetDescendants()) do
            if
                (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox"))
                and obj.Text
                and obj.Text ~= ""
            then
                local text = obj.Text
                if
                    not string.find(text, "Template")
                    and not string.find(text, "Background")
                    and not string.find(text, "Frame")
                    and not string.find(text, "Scroll")
                    and not string.find(text, "Title")
                    and not string.find(text, "Label")
                then
                    if string.match(text, "%a") and #text > 1 and #text < 50 and not string.find(text, "^%d+$") then
                        if not title or (#text > #title) then
                            title = text
                        end
                    end
                    if
                        string.find(text, "%$")
                        or string.find(text, "Cookie")
                        or string.find(text, "Milki")
                        or string.find(text, "coins")
                        or string.find(text, "Cash")
                        or (string.match(text, "^%d+$") and tonumber(text) and tonumber(text) > 50)
                    then
                        cash = text
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if title or cash then
            if title and title ~= "" then
                title = title:gsub("^[%s]+", ""):gsub("[%s]+$", "")
            end
            if cash and cash ~= "" then
                cash = cash:gsub("^[%s]+", ""):gsub("[%s]+$", "")
            end
            if title and string.match(title, "^%d+$") and not cash then
                return
            end
            table.insert(
                list,
                { title = title and title ~= "" and title or "Unknown Item", cash = cash and cash ~= "" and cash or "0" }
            )
        end
    end
    local function processAll(container)
        if not container then
            return
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Frame") and #child:GetChildren() > 0 then
                local hasText = false
                for _, desc in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
                    if
                        (desc:IsA("TextLabel") or desc:IsA("TextButton") or desc:IsA("TextBox"))
                        and desc.Text
                        and desc.Text ~= ""
                    then
                        hasText = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                if hasText then
                    processItem(child)
                end
            end
            if child:IsA("Frame") or child:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
                processAll(child)
            end
        end
    end

        n = tonumber(n) or 0
        if s == "K" or s == "k" then n *= 1e3
        elseif s == "M" or s == "m" then n *= 1e6
        elseif s == "B" or s == "b" then n *= 1e9
        elseif s == "T" or s == "t" then n *= 1e12 end
        return n
    
end)(gen.Text)
                if not bestVal or val > bestVal then
                    bestVal = val
                    bestSG = sg
                    local dn = sg:FindFirstChild("DisplayName", true)
                    best = dn and dn.Text or v.Name
                end
            end
        end

                local mutName, mutMulti, mutColor, traitName, traitMulti, traitSymbol =(function(sg, brainrotName)

        local traitName = nil

        for _, child in ipairs(sg:GetDescendants()) do
            if child:IsA("TextLabel") or child:IsA("TextButton") then
                local text = child.Text:lower()
                local rawText = child.Text
            
                -- Only count if this text is part of the brainrot's info (not opponent, not player name)
                -- Skip if it's clearly a player name or opponent info
                local isPlayerName = false
                for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if rawText == plr.Name or rawText == plr.DisplayName then
                        isPlayerName = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            
                if not isPlayerName then
                    -- Check for mutations
                    for key, data in pairs(MUTATIONS) do
                        if text:find(key) then
                            mutationName = data.name
                            mutationMulti = data.multi
                            mutationColor = data.color
                        end
                    end
                
                    -- Check for traits (must be in brainrot-related context)
                    for key, data in pairs(TRAITS) do
                        if text:find(key) then
                            traitName = data.name
                            traitMulti = data.multi
                            traitSymbol = data.symbol
                        end
                    end
                end
            
                -- Check element names for trait/mutation labels
                local elemName = child.Name:lower()
                for key, data in pairs(MUTATIONS) do
                    if elemName:find(key) then
                        mutationName = data.name
                        mutationMulti = data.multi
                        mutationColor = data.color
                    end
                end
                for key, data in pairs(TRAITS) do
                    if elemName:find(key) then
                        traitName = data.name
                        traitMulti = data.multi
                        traitSymbol = data.symbol
                    end
                end
            end
        
            -- Check ImageLabel for mutation/trait icons (visual effects)
            if child:IsA("ImageLabel") and child.Visible then
                local imgId = tostring(child.Image):lower()
                for key, data in pairs(MUTATIONS) do
                    if imgId:find(key) then
                        mutationName = data.name
                        mutationMulti = data.multi
                        mutationColor = data.color
                    end
                end
                for key, data in pairs(TRAITS) do
                    if imgId:find(key) then
                        traitName = data.name
                        traitMulti = data.multi
                        traitSymbol = data.symbol
                    end
                end
            end
        
            -- Check BillboardGui for overhead trait symbols
            if child:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                for _, billChild in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
                    if billChild:IsA("TextLabel") then
                        local billText = billChild.Text:lower()
                        for key, data in pairs(TRAITS) do
                            if billText:find(key) then
                                traitName = data.name
                                traitMulti = data.multi
                                traitSymbol = data.symbol
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    -- Check for trait images in BillboardGui
                    if billChild:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                        local billImg = tostring(billChild.Image):lower()
                        for key, data in pairs(TRAITS) do
                            if billImg:find(key) then
                                traitName = data.name
                                traitMulti = data.multi
                                traitSymbol = data.symbol
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    
        -- Second pass: if still no trait found, check if brainrot name itself hints at trait
        if not traitName and brainrotName then
            local lowerBrainrot = brainrotName:lower()
            for key, data in pairs(TRAITS) do
                if lowerBrainrot:find(key) then
                    traitName = data.name
                    traitMulti = data.multi
                    traitSymbol = data.symbol
                end
            end
        end
    
        return mutationName, mutationMulti, mutationColor, traitName, traitMulti, traitSymbol
    
end)(bestSG, best)
                local oppName, oppDisplay =(function()

        local oppName = nil
        local oppDisplay = nil
    
        -- Method 1: Check Players service for other players in duel server
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= lp then
                oppName = player.Name
                oppDisplay = player.DisplayName
                break
            end
        end
    
        -- Method 2: Scan workspace for opponent indicators
        if not oppName then
            for _, v in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("TextLabel") or v:IsA("TextButton") then
                    local text = v.Text
                    local lowerText = text:lower()
                
                    if text ~= "" and not text:find(lp.Name) and not text:find(lp.DisplayName) then
                        if v.Name:lower():find("opponent") or v.Name:lower():find("enemy") 
                           or v.Name:lower():find("loser") or v.Name:lower():find("vs") 
                           or v.Name:lower():find("player2") or v.Name:lower():find("rival") then
                            oppName = text
                        end
                    
                        if not oppName and #text > 2 and #text < 50 then
                            if not text:find("%s") and not text:match("^[%d%.]+[KkMmBbTt]?$") 
                               and not lowerText:find("coins") and not lowerText:find("generation")
                               and not lowerText:find("displayname") and not lowerText:find("level")
                               and not lowerText:find("lv") and not lowerText:find("plot") then
                                for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                    if plr ~= lp and (plr.Name == text or plr.DisplayName == text) then
                                        oppName = plr.Name
                                        oppDisplay = plr.DisplayName
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    
        -- Method 3: Check leaderstats
        if not oppName then
            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= lp then
                    local leaderstats = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
                    if leaderstats then
                        oppName = player.Name
                        oppDisplay = player.DisplayName
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    
        return oppName, oppDisplay
    
end)()
